## HF-7 — Windows staged Stop: stop-root-retired not reproduced after HF-1, no phase sample (16 November 2026)

HF-7 was to find the cause of the intermittent Windows x64 staged Stop failure `stop-root-retired` / `core_failure:no_ui_tree`, but only if HF-4's decision table showed a staged Stop failure after HF-1 that carries a UIA phase sample. It does not, so under its brief HF-7 ends with a documented non-reproduction and no code change. It started at `bfeef4a9c73412f0c0e7ac94309924afb93bc9f4`, which equalled `origin/hands-finish`. At write time (about 10:30 UTC on 16 November), `origin/main` was `b362fdf6`, the PR #33 merge committed at 09:23Z, and `origin/release-finish` was `67ec3a60`. Merging `main` into this branch is the lead's step, so HF-7 merged nothing. It changed no file under `packages/` or `.github/` and dispatched nothing. Under its step brief this record was first left uncommitted in the lane worktree. Its commit gate then committed this record, its deviation entry and the note correction as one docs-only commit, titled `docs(hands)` instead of the gate's `fix(hands): resolve …` title, because no Stop failure was resolved and no phase was sampled. Line numbers are at `bfeef4a9`. [Deviation entry](./hands-deviations.md#hf-7--staged-stop-gate-not-met-stop-root-retired-stays-unsampled).

**Gate.**

- HF-4's decision table (staged Stop row) and the evidence file's `decisionGates.hf7` and staged Stop decision ([hands-windows-validation-2026-11-14.json](./evidence/hands-windows-validation-2026-11-14.json)) both say that HF-7 does not proceed.
- The brief asks for "an immutable UIA phase sample". `decisionGates.hf7` asks for "a staged Stop failure with a uia-\* stage". The brief's wording is narrower: a UIA phase stage is the immutable timeout sample when the actor was retired and the live phase when it was healthy, and the result line does not say which. Neither kind occurred.
- In this record a UIA phase stage means a stage that `overlay_driver_uia_stage` can return: `uia-root-current` through `uia-actor-com-expired`, mapped from `WindowsUiaPhase` index 1 onward (`overlay_driver.cpp:499-503`). `uia-create` and `uia-available` also start with `uia-`, but they are closed driver stages (`overlay_driver.h:64`), set at `overlay_driver.cpp:279` and `:281`, and carry no phase.
- The only staged Stop failure at a head that contains HF-1 is run [43813017303](https://github.com/scr1pter/claudeondfsfdaf/actions/runs/43813017303), x64 job `133123907277`, at `289af6bb`: `{"protocol":1,"kind":"result","phase":"exit","stop":{"ok":false,"reason":"cleanup_failed","stage":"observe-up","cause":"driver_failure"},"exitCode":1}` at 06:24:25.3656124Z. `observe-up` is a closed driver stage after posted input; see below.
- The `f830e3fd` failure is run [43631024090](https://github.com/scr1pter/claudeondfsfdaf/actions/runs/43631024090), job `132558654772`: `{"protocol":1,"kind":"result","phase":"exit","stop":{"ok":false,"reason":"native_failure","stage":"stop-root-retired","cause":"core_failure:no_ui_tree"},"exitCode":1}` at 19:34:47.3208342Z on 14 November. That source predates `8e792a72` and `503418a1`, so it has no sample. HF-7 read that job's log again. Between the controller `native-proof` line (19:34:45.4064812Z) and the result line, the only line is `computer-smoke-asar`. Nothing in the log says which root observation failed or how the actor was retired.

**Re-gate check at write time.**

- `list_workflow_runs` for `test.yml` on `hands-finish` returned three runs (`total_count` 3), all `workflow_dispatch`, attempt 1, at `289af6bb`. `git merge-base --is-ancestor acd0c9bb 289af6bb` holds, so all three contain the HF-1 merge. HF-5's two dispatches had not run.
- Each of the three used `computer_helper_target=windows`. In each run the `computer-helper-windows-units` job, the separate baseline and move-probe matrix (`test.yml:169-176`, staged steps at `:361-388`), was skipped: jobs `133106699615`, `133118954234` and `133130361807`. The only staged Stop in each run is windows-build step 18, "Run actual isolated medium-token Windows overlay Stop" (`computer-helper.yml:797-808`).
- That step sets `VECTOR_COMPUTER_TEST_ISOLATED=1` and `PRIVILEGED_REQUIRED=false` and no `VECTOR_COMPUTER_SMOKE_MOVE_PROBE`; the logged env blocks of all three jobs agree. The driver reads a missing variable as off (`overlay_driver.cpp:81-87`, `:453-456`), so all three are baseline Stops with no MOVE before the refresh. N is 3.
- No line printed `stop-root-retired`, `stop-root-refused`, `stop-hit-retired` or `stop-hit-refused`, a UIA phase stage, `uia-create` or `uia-available`.

**What the code records** (code reading at `bfeef4a9`, not CI evidence).

- A baseline Stop makes two root observations before any pointer input: the first at `overlay_driver.cpp:284`, and the refresh's at `:305`, through `refresh_stop` (`:255-256`). The MOVE probe (`:292-304`) runs only with `VECTOR_COMPUTER_SMOKE_MOVE_PROBE=1`. Both line numbers are the same at `f830e3fd`.
- Each root observation is one `call()` on the UIA actor, with its own 50 ms ticket (`uia_native.cpp:277`). `backend.root` notes `root-current` and checks the window before the call (`:627`). `call()` notes `actor-admission` (`:278`), refuses at admission when the actor is closed or quarantined or already has 8 jobs (`:279`), and notes `actor-queued` (`:280`) before it waits. The first `call()` on a new actor also runs the one-time COM/UIA setup in `prepare` (`:214-238`); later calls return from `prepare` after `prepare-desktop` (`:215`). A stage from `uia-prepare-create` to `uia-prepare-property` can therefore come only from the first observation.
- `observe_stop` (`overlay_driver.cpp:212-228`) classifies a root failure as `stop-root-retired` when `available()` is false and `stop-root-refused` when it is true (`:222`), then refines the stage from `failure_phase(available)` (`:224`). A hit failure follows the same pattern (`:229-241`). `available()` is false only when the actor is closed or quarantined (`uia_native.cpp:267`). click_stop closes the actor only in its `Close` destructor (`overlay_driver.cpp:280`), which runs after `observe_stop`'s catch has classified, so inside that catch a false `available()` means quarantined.
- For a retired actor, `failure_phase(false)` returns only the immutable timeout sample (`uia_native.h:50-55`). `note_timeout` (`:45-49`) writes that sample once, when `actor-expired` or `actor-com-expired` is noted (`uia_native.cpp:38`). A healthy actor reports its live phase. `overlay_driver_uia_stage` returns no stage for `none` (`overlay_driver.cpp:499-503`), and then the stage stays plain.
- Inside click_stop the actor can be quarantined in two ways:
  1. A ticket expiry in `call()` (`uia_native.cpp:289-292`). `note(actor_expired)` samples the phase before the quarantine at `:291`. A root observation retired this way prints `uia-<phase>` at `bfeef4a9`, and for a root call the sampled phase is `actor-queued` or a later one.
  2. An actor-thread startup quarantine (`uia_native.cpp:250`). `NativeUia::make` starts the actor thread detached and does not wait for it (`:245-263`). If `CoInitializeEx` or `DuplicateHandle` fails, the thread sets `quarantined` without calling `note_timeout`. The `uia-available` check (`overlay_driver.cpp:281`) can run before the thread gets that far. If the quarantine is in place when the first root `call()` reaches admission, admission throws `no_ui_tree` (`uia_native.cpp:279`), `available()` is false and `failure_phase(false)` returns `none`. The line is then a plain `stop-root-retired` / `core_failure:no_ui_tree`, with no refinement. If the quarantine lands after admission, the thread's wait (`:253`) discards the queued job unrun, `call()`'s own `promise` (`:276`) keeps the future pending, and the caller's ticket expires after 50 ms. That writes the sample with phase `actor-queued` (`note(actor_expired)` at `:290`, through `note_timeout` at `:38`), so the line prints `uia-actor-queued`. A `uia-actor-queued` root stage therefore does not separate a startup quarantine from a scheduling expiry. Either ordering can only affect the first observation, because the refresh follows a first `call()` that the thread completed.
- So at `bfeef4a9` a plain `stop-root-retired` means "retired with no timeout sample". The only such route this reading found is a startup quarantine already in place when the first root `call()` reaches admission; HF-7 does not claim the list is complete or say how likely either route is. A refined root stage does not say whether it is a sample or a live phase, or which observation failed.
- At `f830e3fd` the driver refined only a healthy actor (`if (available && diagnostic)` at `overlay_driver.cpp:224`), and the startup quarantine is at `uia_native.cpp:247`. A retirement by either route printed the same plain `stop-root-retired` there, and the ticket-expiry route could come from either observation. The `f830e3fd` line therefore shows neither which root observation failed nor whether the actor was retired by a ticket expiry or by a startup quarantine.

- Each tested merge is the `computer-helper.yml` entry of the run's `referenced_workflows` at `refs/pull/33/merge`, as HF-4 recorded for the first three. Every PR run in the table was created after `58f85edc` (18:32Z on 14 November; run 43631024090 at 19:14:44Z) and before PR #33 merged, so `main` was `58f85edc` for each.
- On the pre-sample source, the staged Stop failed once in seven recorded runs, at `f830e3fd`.

**The `observe-up` failure.** In run 43813017303 the driver reached `observe-up`, so the guardian had confirmed the DOWN: an unconfirmed DOWN fails at `observe-down` or at a `guardian-observe-*` refinement (`overlay_driver.cpp:315-319`). After `guardian->retire(*ticket)`, `guardian->observed(*ticket, false, 300)` returned false and `require(confirmed_up, L"cleanup_failed")` threw (`:322`). `observe-up` has no guardian-phase refinement, unlike `observe-down` at `:316-317`, so the line does not show whether the guardian timed out or refused.

- `NativeGuardian::observed` returns false at once for an invalid request (`input_guardian.cpp:468-470`).
- Through `exchange` it can also fail with a closed phase: `lock_expired` (`:566`), `budget_expired` (`:570`, `:589`), `inactive` (`:588`, `:590`), or `setup_failed`, `write_refused`, `read_refused` and `reply_invalid` (`:593-599`). A negative reply sets one of the `reply_negative_*` phases (`:476-490`). Not all of these come at once: `lock_expired` follows a wait of up to the 50 ms budget for the control lock, `budget_expired` means that budget had already run out in the lock wait or the retirement flush (`:569`), and the transfers behind `write_refused` and `read_refused` (`:596`, `:598`) fail at once on a pipe error or cancellation, or when the deadline cancels a blocked transfer. `inactive`, `setup_failed` and `reply_invalid` take no wait of their own.
- Whatever the caller passes, it caps the exchange at 50 ms (`std::min<uint32_t>(timeout,50)`, `:473`), and that budget also covers flushing the retirement just queued (`:569`). The 300 in the call is not the wait.
- It stores the phase in the guardian diagnostic (`:493`), but click_stop does not read it at `observe-up`.
- It is not a UIA or root failure: both root observations, the hit and the refresh had passed. The post-Stop `native-os-stop` check failed with `native_proof_invalid`. It stays outside HF-7.

**Why no change.** The brief allows a fix only for what a sampled phase shows, and no sampled phase exists. A different 50 ms budget, a warm-up or retry, a replacement actor, or a change to what Stop must prove before it acts would each be a guess, and the brief requires a deviation entry and lead review for each. The `1558c15b` and `601c17d0` proof requirements and the retired versus refused classification stay as they are. No skip or inferred success is added.

**HF-8 record lines** (verbatim):

- (1) "Windows x64 staged Stop stop-root-retired / core_failure:no_ui_tree: observed once on the PR #33 native source, at f830e3fd (run 43631024090, job 132558654772, 19:34:47Z on 14 November), which has no immutable UIA timeout sample. Older driver sources, such as the stop-hit-retired failures recorded up to 9 November, are not counted here. The line shows neither which root observation failed nor whether the actor was retired by a 50 ms ticket expiry or by an actor-thread startup quarantine (uia_native.cpp:247 at that source), which takes no sample. At heads that contain HF-1, a startup quarantine that lands after the first root call() is admitted discards the queued job and lets that call's ticket expire, so the line prints uia-actor-queued; a uia-actor-queued root stage therefore does not separate a startup quarantine from a scheduling expiry. Not reproduced in 3 baseline test.yml windows dispatches at heads that contain HF-1 (runs 43809140577, 43813017303 and 43816713717, all at 289af6bb); none printed a stop-root-\* or stop-hit-\* stage or a UIA phase stage, so HF-7's gate was not met and no code changed. The same pre-sample source passed the staged Stop in six other recorded runs. Cause still unidentified."
- (2) "Windows x64 staged Stop observe-up: 1 of 3 baseline Stops at heads that contain HF-1 failed cleanup_failed / observe-up / driver_failure (run 43813017303, job 133123907277, at 289af6bb). After a confirmed DOWN, guardian->observed(ticket, false, 300) returned false (overlay_driver.cpp:322) and require(confirmed_up, cleanup_failed) threw. observe-up has no guardian-phase refinement, unlike observe-down at :316-317, so the line does not show whether the guardian timed out or refused. A different failure from stop-root-retired, after posted input. Outside HF-7; cause still unidentified; the lead decides."

If HF-5's runs add staged Stop results before HF-8 runs, HF-8 restates the counts and run lists in both lines from them.

**Proposals for the lead (not built).** Each would be its own package with a deviation entry. Both change the Windows source digest, because `sourceDigestFor` hashes every file under `windows/`. Neither changes the macOS helper bytes, `helperVersion` `1.0.15` or invariant 2.

- (a) Make a refined root or hit stage say whether it is a retired sample or a live phase, and whether it came from the first observation or the refresh. That grows the 172-entry `overlay_driver_stages` catalog (`overlay_driver.h:61`) and `COMPUTER_SMOKE_DRIVER_STAGES` (`computer-overlay-smoke-report.ts:406`, not a build input). It can stay in the never-shipped driver, which only tests and the dedicated driver link (`windows/CMakeLists.txt:91-93`). Naming a startup quarantine on its own, before or after admission, would need a closed phase in `uia_native.cpp`, which is compiled into `computer-core` (`:85`), so the shipped Windows helper bytes would change too.
- (b) At an `observe-up` failure, sample `guardian_diagnostic->phase` through `overlay_driver_guardian_stage`, as `observe-down` does at `:317`. The existing `guardian-observe-*` stages suffice, and the reason (`cleanup_failed` versus `input_post_failed`) keeps UP and DOWN apart. It touches only the never-shipped `overlay_driver.cpp`, changes no budget, and leaves the shipped helper bytes unchanged.

## HF-7 (1) phase sample — uia-root-element at 66164bf9, diagnostics only (16 November 2026)

The lane PR's run [43907070848](https://github.com/scr1pter/claudeondfsfdaf/actions/runs/43907070848) tested merge `66164bf9` ("Merge 7078232f into 2034e1da"). Its windows-build job `133443621242` failed the step "Run actual isolated medium-token Windows overlay Stop": `native-proof` at 16:49:39.8003910Z, `computer-smoke-asar` at 16:49:39.8056833Z, then at 16:49:41.8273728Z `{"protocol":1,"kind":"result","phase":"exit","stop":{"ok":false,"reason":"native_failure","stage":"uia-root-element","cause":"core_failure:no_ui_tree"},"exitCode":1}`. The report then had `native-os-stop` and `native-capture-exclusion` as `native_proof_invalid`, and the step threw "Actual isolated overlay Stop failed or required native evidence is unavailable." Every other check passed, including window-region 4/4 and every controller step; the bootstrap token route was `restricted`, as in passing jobs. The Stop test body ran, so this is not re-run as a flake.

This is the first UIA phase sample for HF-7 (1). Before HF-1 the same event printed `stop-root-retired` (`f830e3fd`, run 43631024090). The label comes from hands-finish's own refinement (`failure_phase(available)`, `overlay_driver.cpp:220-224` at `7078232f`): on a healthy actor it is the live phase, and on a retired actor the immutable timeout snapshot. On `main` the same event prints `stop-root-retired` if the actor was retired and `uia-root-element` only if it was healthy. Ok, reason and exit 1 are the same on both.

**What the line does and does not show.** The worker had announced `root_element` (`uia_native.cpp:365` at `7078232f`: `note(root_element); current_call();` then `ElementFromHandleBuildCache`) and `unavailable()` threw `Failure{no_ui_tree}`. It does not show:

- whether the HRESULT failed on a healthy actor or the 50 ms ticket (`:277`) expired and the caller retired the actor (`:289-292`);
- whether it was the first root lookup (`overlay_driver.cpp:284`) or the refresh (`:305`); both start at `stop_root`;
- on a retired actor, whether `ElementFromHandleBuildCache` was ever called. A retired `root_element` sample is an observation at timeout, not proof that the provider call caused the delay ([diagnostic note](./hands-uia-timeout-diagnostic.md#first-phase-sample-uia-root-element-16-november-2026)).

**The branch did not cause it.** The branch's native diff before this entry touches only failure paths: `note_timeout` runs only once a deadline has passed and `failure_phase` is read only in the catch. Its `computer-helper.yml` change touches only the summary steps, and HF-5's `settle_input_detection` runs only in the privileged self-test. `git diff a0e2128f 7078232f` and `git diff d9d5afaf 7078232f` show nothing under `packages/desktop/native`, and that native source passed the Stop in jobs `133421895080` (`a0e2128f`) and `133333996211` (`d9d5afaf`).

**Runs read on 16 November** (saved log tails; native-proof to the result frame):

| windows-build job | Head                      | Staged Stop                                                               | native-proof to result |
| ----------------- | ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------- |
| `133286255135`    | `d9d5afaf` (HF-5 run 1)   | `cleanup_failed` / `observe-up` / `driver_failure`, exit 1                | 2.83 s                 |
| `133301760101`    | `main` `1b1563dd`         | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 2.18 s                 |
| `133327849581`    | PR #39 `b6370e89`         | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 2.14 s                 |
| `133331740561`    | PR #39 `b6370e89`         | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 2.35 s                 |
| `133333996211`    | `d9d5afaf` (HF-5 run 2)   | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 2.29 s                 |
| `133342281087`    | native-qualify `2677c32c` | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 1.85 s                 |
| `133353004287`    | `main` `33fc9fbc`         | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 2.30 s                 |
| `133372821916`    | connect-finish `ec51507b` | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 1.85 s                 |
| `133401774621`    | `main` `2034e1da`         | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 1.54 s                 |
| `133421895080`    | hands-finish `a0e2128f`   | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 2.19 s                 |
| `133430545659`    | codium-finish `0b9f8337`  | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 2.18 s                 |
| `133443621242`    | PR #40 merge `66164bf9`   | `native_failure` / `uia-root-element` / `core_failure:no_ui_tree`, exit 1 | 2.03 s                 |
| `133467844726`    | connect-finish `1d944dd9` | `ok: true`, exit 0                                                        | 1.87 s                 |

Tally over these 13: one `uia-root-element`, one `observe-up` (HF-7 (2)), 11 passes. None of the passing jobs printed a `uia-*` or `stop-root-*` stage. The failing job's 2.03 s lies inside the passes' 1.54 to 2.35 s, so timing does not separate pass from fail.

**Diagnostics added (no behaviour change).** After merging `main` `3ab7858e` (merge `ef44107d`), a failed Stop root or hit lookup now adds one closed `uia` object beside `stage` and `cause`: `lookup` (`first` from `overlay_driver.cpp` click_stop, `refresh` from `refresh_stop`), `call` (`root` or `hit`), `actor` (`healthy` or `retired`, the bit the stage refinement used to overwrite), `expiry` (`none`, `worker-current`, `worker-budget` or `caller-wait`) and `hresult` (`unobserved` or one of the existing `overlay_driver_hresult_classes`). `expiry` and the root status are written into the same first-write timeout snapshot as the phase, before cancellation, so late work after `CoCancelCall` cannot change them; a healthy actor reports its live status, a retired one only the snapshot. Nothing is written on the success path: the side is written only in the three expiry branches and the status only when `ElementFromHandleBuildCache` fails. `Failure{no_ui_tree}`, the 50 ms budgets, the freshness checks, ok, reason and exit are unchanged. [Deviation entry](./hands-deviations.md#hf-7-1--closed-uia-lookup-facts-for-the-uia-root-element-stop-16-november-2026).

**Reading the next sample.**

| `actor`   | `expiry`         | `hresult`    | Reading                                                                                                                                     |
| --------- | ---------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `retired` | `worker-current` | `unobserved` | The worker found the budget spent at the check before `ElementFromHandleBuildCache`; the call was never made. Points at fix (a) on `first`. |
| `retired` | `caller-wait`    | `unobserved` | The provider call was in flight when the caller woke. Points at fix (b), or at (c) on `refresh`.                                            |
| `retired` | `caller-wait`    | a bucket     | The call returned that failure after the deadline but before retirement.                                                                    |
| `healthy` | `none`           | a bucket     | The call failed within its budget; the bucket names the class.                                                                              |

`caller-wait` does not say how much of the 50 ms earlier steps used, only that the deadline passed after the worker announced the call.

**Status.** HF-7 (1) stays open with its cause unidentified. A green next run is not evidence of a fix: this failure appeared in 1 of the 13 Stops above. Fixes (a) to (c) each need lead review under the HF-7 brief, and the lead decides whether PR #40 merges with this known intermittent or waits for one. The Windows source digest changes again (`uia_native.*`, `overlay_driver.*`); `helperVersion` stays `1.0.15` (HF-1 precedent) and the new source needs Windows re-qualification: native units, isolated Stop, x64 and ARM64.

**Checks.** From `packages/desktop`, through the serial lock, one file at a time: `scripts/computer-overlay-smoke-report.test.ts` and `scripts/computer-overlay-smoke-source.test.ts` passed, and `bun typecheck` passed. The C++ groups in `tests/overlay_driver_tests.cpp` build and run only in CI.

## HF-6 diagnostic package — closed affinity and listener-health stages (21 November 2026)

Branch `hands-native-diag`, from `main` `5a5b5619`; not pushed when this was written. The [deviation entry](./hands-deviations.md#hf-6-diagnostic--closed-first-stale-affinity-terms-and-listener-health-classes-21-november-2026) has the change, the outcome table and its limit.

- **What it prints.** ARM64 `capture-affinity-exclusion` at planning now prints `affinity-plan-current-<term>` (16 closed terms) instead of `affinity-plan-current`, and a plan that goes stale during the display read prints `affinity-capture-stale-<term>` where the row already failed at `affinity-native-sample-missing`. `input-listener-health` at its one re-check prints one of `input-health-unavailable-listener`, `-unavailable-context`, `-calibrating`, `-no-foreground`, `-foreground-refused` or `-observe-slow`, or `input-health-ready` when the facts are clean. The stage catalog grows from 224 to 261.
- **What did not change.** Budgets, predicates, retries, authority, pass/skip/exit codes, `continue-on-error`, `user_input.cpp`, the Dispatcher, `UserInputService`, the listener, `helperVersion` `1.0.15`, `macos/`, `identity.json` and `blocklist.json`.
- **Checks.** From `packages/desktop`, through the serial lock: `scripts/computer-helper-selftest.test.ts` passed (51 tests), with two tests red when only `privileged_self_test.cpp` is reverted; `bun typecheck` passed. The pure functions and the two new native groups passed a local g++ stub harness, with two mutations red. MSVC, the native units and the isolated rows are CI-only.
- **Pending.** The PR run's windows-build (MSVC `/W4 /WX` x64 and ARM64, native units, x64 isolated rows expected at 37/0/11) and windows-arm64-test, or a `test.yml` dispatch with `computer_helper=true` and `computer_helper_target=windows` if the PR run does not reach ARM64. The printed ARM64 terms are recorded here and in the deviation entry when they exist; until then HF-6's cause stays unidentified.
- **HF-5 trigger.** No `user_input.cpp` change; the row 16 facts are the trigger evidence. The lead is asked to rule the HF-5 trigger item moot (deviation entry, point 8).
